Works both as an activation cost and as a SPELL-slot additional cast cost ("As an additional cost to cast this spell, pay 3 life" — Fumarole); the cast is rejected when the life total is below the amount (CR 119.4)
- `RepeatableAdditionalManaCost(List<String> manaCosts)` — SPELL-slot "you may pay {1}{R} and/or {1}{G} any number of times" (Primitive Justice). One announced entry per repetition; paid as a mana suffix on the spell's total cost. When the payments buy targets, announced X must be `1 + repetitions`; pair with `targetX(filter, cap)`. Without an X-scaled target group (Taste of Paradise) X is unconstrained. Read the per-option payment count back with the `RepeatedAdditionalCostCount("{1}{G}")` amount
- `PayXLifeCost()` — SPELL-slot additional cast cost: pay X life, where X is the announced X (Fire Covenant)
- `ExileCardFromGraveyardCost(CardType)` / `(CardSubtype)` + overloads — exile graveyard card (subtype ctor for "Exile an Elf card", Scarred Vinebreeder)
- `ReturnCreatureToHandCost()` — additional spell cost: return a creature you control to hand (Familiar's Ruse)
- `PutCounterOnControlledCreatureCost(CounterType, int count)` — additional spell cost: put counter(s) on a creature you control (Scarscale Ritual: `MINUS_ONE_MINUS_ONE, 1`); creature supplied via `sacrificePermanentId`, paid in `SpellCastingService`

See EFFECTS_INDEX.md "Sacrifice costs" for additional cost effects.
- `DestroyDamagedCreatureEffect()` — `ON_ALLY_CREATURE_DEALS_DAMAGE_TO_CREATURE` marker, self-scoped: fires only when the permanent holding it is the damage source. Expanded at trigger-collection time into a `DestroyTargetPermanentEffect` entry aimed at the damaged creature, so it never resolves directly. Granted to itself by Cruel Deceiver
- `SacrificeAnyNumberOfPermanentsCost(PermanentPredicate filter)` — `CostEffect`; SPELL-slot additional cast cost "you may sacrifice any number of permanents you control matching `filter`". Zero is a legal payment, so it never blocks castability; the count sacrificed becomes the spell's X. Rides on `PlayCardRequest.additionalCostSacrificePermanentIds`. Devouring Greed pairs it with `Sum(Fixed(2), Scaled(XValue(), 2))` life loss. Contrast `SacrificeMultiplePermanentsCost` (exact count) and `SacrificeXPermanentsCost` (activated abilities only)

## Counter spells

- `CounterSpellEffect()` — counter target spell (countered card → graveyard)
- `CounterAbilityAndLockSourceEffect(EffectDuration)` — counter the targeted ability and lock its source permanent's activated abilities for the duration (Interdict)
- `CounterSpellEffect(CounteredSpellDestination.EXILE)` — counter + exile instead of graveyard (Dissipate, Faerie Trickery)
- `CounterSpellEffect(CounteredSpellDestination.LIBRARY_TOP)` — counter + put on top of owner's library instead of graveyard (Memory Lapse)
- `CounterEachTargetSpellEffect()` — "counter up to N target spells" (Double Negative). Bind to one `target(filter, 0, N)` spell-on-stack group; counters every spell chosen for the group. `null` filter = any spell. Non-modal multi-spell-target path (see `SpellCastingService.multipleSpellTargets`)
- `CounterSpellAndCreateTreasureTokensEffect()` — counter + treasures
- `CounterSpellAndExileAllWithSameNameEffect()` — counter + exile all same-name cards from controller's graveyard/hand/library, then shuffle (Counterbore)
- `CounterSpellIfNameFoundElsewhereEffect()` — ON_ANY_PLAYER_CASTS_SPELL: counter the triggering spell if a card with the same name is in a graveyard or a nontoken permanent with the same name is on the battlefield (Bazaar of Wonders)
- `CounterSpellIfControllerPoisonedEffect()` — counter if poisoned
- `TargetSpellControllerLosesLifeEffect(int)` — target spell controller loses life (a `TriggeringSpellReferencingEffect`: also usable in `ON_BECOMES_TARGET_OF_SPELL`, where it hits the triggering spell's controller — Forsaken Wastes)
- `TargetSpellControllerDiscardsEffect(int)` — target spell controller discards
- `TargetSpellControllerDrawsCardEffect()` — target spell controller draws a card; place before the counter (Dream Fracture)
- `TargetSpellControllerGainsLifeEqualToManaValueEffect()` — target spell controller gains life equal to that spell's mana value (including X); place before the counter (Illumination)
- `CounterUnlessPaysEffect(int)` or `(int amount, int lifeCost)` or `(int, boolean useX, boolean exileIfCountered)` or `(DynamicAmount)` — counter unless pays (`DynamicAmount` scales the cost, e.g. `PermanentCount(PermanentColorInPredicate(BLUE), CONTROLLER)` = "{1} for each blue permanent you control", Spell Syphon). `(amount, lifeCost)` is mana **and** life (Mundungu)
- `CounterUnlessDiscardsEffect()` — counter unless controller discards a card (Ward—Discard a card)
- `CounterSpellsNamedLikeCardsExiledWithSourceEffect()` — non-targeting: counter all stack spells named like a card exiled with the source (Grimoire Thief; pair with `SacrificeSelfCost`)
- `ExileOtherSpellsAndCounterAbilitiesEffect()` — non-targeting: exile all other spells on the stack and counter all activated/triggered abilities (Summary Dismissal). Uncounterable spells are still exiled; Guile does not apply
- `CounterSpellAndGainControlIfArtifactOrCreatureEffect()` — counter target spell; if it was an artifact or creature spell, put that card onto the battlefield under your control instead of the graveyard (Desertion)
- `CounterlashEffect()` — counter target spell, then may cast from hand sharing a card type without paying mana cost
- `RegisterDelayedManaEqualToTargetSpellManaValueEffect(ManaColor)` — Scattering Stroke clash reward: wrap in `ClashEffect` before the counter; may add {C} equal to the countered spell's mana value at your next main phase
- `RegisterDelayedManaAtNextMainPhaseEffect(ManaColor, int)` — schedule mandatory fixed mana at the beginning of your next main phase this turn (Conduit of Storms / Emrakul ON_ATTACK)
- `MayCastFromHandWithoutPayingManaCostEffect()` / `(boolean revealCardOnDecline)` — marker for may-cast-from-hand routing in PendingMayAbility; use `false` when declining must not publicly identify a hidden chosen card
- `MayCastForMiracleCostEffect()` / `MiracleMayCastEffect()` / `MiracleRevealEffect()` — Miracle cast path (Temporal Mastery)
- `MayCastForMadnessCostEffect()` / `MadnessMayCastEffect()` — Madness cast path (Asylum Visitor); discard exiles then offers cast-from-exile for madness cost
- `MayCastFromHandSharingNameWithSpellCastThisTurnEffect()` — Twinning Glass activated ability: offer to cast a nonland hand card for free if its name matches a spell any player cast this turn (reuses the Counterlash routing)
- `MayCastAnySpellFromHandWithoutPayingManaCostEffect(CardPredicate spellFilter)` — offer to cast one matching nonland hand card for free (reuses Counterlash routing). `()` / null filter = any nonland (Maelstrom Archangel, ON_COMBAT_DAMAGE_TO_PLAYER); instant/sorcery filter = Wildfire Eternal (ON_ATTACKS_UNBLOCKED)
- `MasterOfPredicamentsEffect()` — combat-damage trigger with a hidden hand-card selection followed by the damaged player's mana-value guess; wrong nonland guesses reuse the free-cast may path
- `MayCastFromTargetPlayerHandWithoutPayingManaCostEffect(CardPredicate spellFilter)` — same, but eligible cards come from the *targeted* player's hand; the controller casts (Mindclaw Shaman; pair with `RevealTargetHandEffect`)
- `ReplaceControlledCounterWithExileAndPlayEffect()` — STATIC (Guile): your counters exile the spell instead and you may play it free
- `MayPlayExiledCardWithoutPayingManaCostEffect()` — marker for free-play-from-exile routing in PendingMayAbility (Guile's counter replacement, Spell Queller's leaves trigger)
- `CantBeCounteredEffect()` — can't be countered (static); `CantBeCounteredEffect(new SpellXAtLeast(5))` — conditional on stack-entry X (Banefire)
- `MakeTargetSpellUncounterableEffect()` — target spell can't be countered (activated/spell; targets a spell on the stack, Vexing Shusher)
- `CreatureSpellsCantBeCounteredEffect()` — creatures can't be countered (static)
- `ControllerCreatureSpellsCantBeCounteredEffect(int minimumPower)` — creature spells you control with power dz?A `minimumPower` can't be countered (static, Spellbreaker Behemoth)
- `CreatureEnteringDontCauseTriggersEffect()` — Torpor Orb (static)
- `ETBDoubleTriggerEffect(CardPredicate)` — double ETB triggers (static)
- `CreaturesEnterAsCopyOfSourceEffect()` — Essence of the Wild (static); `(true, true)` = nontoken creatures enter as a copy of the enchanted creature (Infinite Reflection)
- `OtherNontokenCreaturesBecomeCopyOfEnchantedCreatureEffect()` — Infinite Reflection ETB (ON_ENTER_BATTLEFIELD, no target)
- `ExileOpponentCardsInsteadOfGraveyardEffect()` — Leyline of the Void (static)
- `ExileOpponentCreaturesInsteadOfDyingEffect()` — Liesa, Forgotten Archangel (static); opponents' creatures are exiled instead of dying
- `ExileInstantSorceryCardsInsteadOfGraveyardEffect()` — instant and sorcery cards are exiled instead of entering any graveyard; token objects are unaffected (static, Dryad Militant)
- `RevealAndPutOnBottomOfLibraryInsteadOfGraveyardEffect()` — Wheel of Sun and Moon (static, player aura; cards to enchanted player's graveyard go to bottom of their library instead; pair with `setEnchantPlayer(true)`)
- `ExileOwnCardsInsteadOfGraveyardEffect()` — controller's own cards are exiled instead of going to their graveyard (static, Forbidden Crypt)
- `ExileOwnCyclingCardsUnlessCycledEffect()` — cycling cards are exiled instead of going to your graveyard unless they were just cycled (static, Abandoned Sarcophagus; capability `OwnGraveyardExileReplacement`)
- `CastSpellsWithCyclingFromGraveyardEffect()` — cast spells with cycling from your graveyard (static, Abandoned Sarcophagus; capability `CastSpellsFromGraveyardPermission`)
- `CastSpellFromGraveyardOncePerYourTurnEffect(CardPredicate filter)` — "once during each of your turns, you may cast a [filter] spell from your graveyard" (static, Gisa and Geralf: `CardAllOfPredicate(CardTypePredicate(CREATURE), CardSubtypePredicate(ZOMBIE))`). Same `CastSpellsFromGraveyardPermission` capability with `oncePerControllerTurn() == true`: only usable on its controller's own turn, tracked per granting permanent in `GameData.oncePerTurnGraveyardCastPermissionsUsedThisTurn`
- `ReturnFromGraveyardInsteadOfDrawEffect()` — if you would draw, return a card from your graveyard to hand instead; lose if you can't (static, Forbidden Crypt)
- `EnduringRenewalDrawReplacementEffect()` — if you would draw, reveal top; creature → GY, else draw (static, capability `RevealTopCreatureToGraveyardElseDrawReplacementEffect`)
- `PlayWithOwnHandRevealedEffect()` — play with your hand revealed (controller only; capability `PubliclyRevealedHandEffect`)
- `MakeDefendingPlayerPlayWithHandRevealedEffect()` — the trigger's defending player (`targetId`) plays with their hand revealed for as long as the source (`sourcePermanentId`) stays on the battlefield; recorded in `GameData.handsRevealedWhileSourceOnBattlefield`. `ON_ATTACKS_UNBLOCKED`; pair as `MayEffect(SequenceEffect.of(it, AssignNoCombatDamageEffect()))` (Stromgald Spy)
- `ReturnTriggeringCreatureToOwnerHandEffect()` — ON_ALLY_CREATURE_DIES return dying creature to hand (Enduring Renewal)
- `PutOnTopOfLibraryInsteadOfDyingEffect()` — if this creature would die, put it on top of its owner's library instead (static replacement, Gravebane Zombie)
- `PutControlledCreatureOnTopOfLibraryEffect()` — "put a creature you control on top of its owner's library" (Nulltread Gargantuan ETB). Non-targeted, mandatory choice at resolution; the source itself is a legal pick (and forced when it's the only creature you control). One candidate resolves automatically; several prompt `PermanentChoiceContext.PutControlledCreatureOnTopOfLibrary`. No card target filter
- `CounterSpellIfManaValueEqualsXEffect()` — counters the target spell only if its mana value equals the ability's X, which a `DiscardCardTypeCost` with `trackManaValue` snapshots off the discarded card. Benign `SPELL_ON_STACK` spec. Hisoka, Minamo Sensei

## Bounce / return to hand

- `ReturnToHandEffect` — unified bounce, **static factories only**: `.target()` (bounce target), `.targetAndControllerLosesLife(1)` (Vapor Snag), `.self()` (bounce source permanent), `.selfSpell()` (the resolving instant/sorcery returns itself to its owner's hand off the stack instead of the graveyard — Redeem the Lost's won-clash reward), `.allPermanentsMatching(filter)` (mass bounce matching permanents; null = every permanent — pass `PermanentIsCreaturePredicate` for creatures), `.permanentsTargetPlayerControls(filter)` (River's Rebuke), `.permanentsTargetPlayerOwns(filter)` (Hurkyl's Recall, owner-based), `.aurasAttachedToTarget()` (return every Aura attached to the targeted permanent to its owner's hand, whoever controls the Aura — Scarab of the Unseen; benign `PERMANENT` spec, the Auras themselves aren't targeted), `.enchanted()` (bounce the permanent the source Aura is attached to — Sun Clasp; non-targeting)
- `ReturnTargetAndAttachedMatchingToHandEffect(PermanentPredicate attachedFilter)` — bounce target creature **and** attached permanents matching `attachedFilter` (attachments first so they aren't orphaned). Word of Undoing: `AllOf(Aura, ColorIn(WHITE), OwnedBySourceController)`
- `ReturnTargetSpellToHandEffect()` — bounce target spell from the stack to its owner's hand (not countering; Hullbreaker Horror)
- `ReturnTargetPermanentToHandWithManaValueConditionalEffect(int, CardEffect)` — bounce + MV bonus
- `ReturnTargetPermanentToHandOrLibraryTopByPredicateEffect(PermanentPredicate)` — bounce to hand, or to top of library instead when target matches predicate (Consign to Dream)
- `ReturnSelfToHandOnCoinFlipLossEffect()` — bounce self on coin flip loss
- `ReturnPermanentsOnCombatDamageToPlayerEffect()` or `(PermanentPredicate)` — Ninja-style
- `PutTargetOnBottomOfLibraryEffect()` — tuck bottom
- `PutTargetOnTopOfLibraryEffect()` — tuck top; `.self()` tucks the source instead; `new PutTargetOnTopOfLibraryEffect(PutOnTopOfLibraryScope.SELF_AND_TARGET)` tucks the source AND the target creature, then shuffles both owners' libraries (Void Stalker)
- `PutTargetCreatureOnTopOrOptionalBottomOfLibraryEffect(PermanentPredicate)` — tuck target creature on top, but if it matches the predicate the controller may put it on the bottom instead (Ether Well, `PermanentColorInPredicate(Set.of(RED))`)
- `PutTargetPermanentIntoLibraryNFromTopEffect(int)` — tuck N from top
- `PutTargetSpellOrPermanentIntoLibraryNFromTopEffect(int)` — tuck target spell or nonland permanent N from top (Commit = 1; not a counter)
- `PutSourceCardFromGraveyardIntoLibraryNFromTopEffect(position)` — ON_DEATH: put dying source into owner's library N from top (0=top: Undying Beast; 2=third: Enigma Sphinx)
- `ExileSourceCardFromGraveyardEffect()` — ON_DEATH: exile dying source from owner's graveyard ("When this dies, exile it", Cyclopean Mummy)
- `ExileIfHadCounterElseReturnWithCounterEffect(CounterType)` — ON_DEATH: exile if dying source had that counter, else return self to battlefield under your control with one of that counter (Bogardan Phoenix / death counters). Collector snapshots the counter at death and bakes `ExileSourceCardFromGraveyardEffect` or self `ReturnCardFromGraveyardEffect` with `enterWithCounter`
- `ReturnMatchingPermanentsUnlessOwnerPaysEffect(PermanentPredicate filter, String manaCost)` — `BoardWipeEffect`; punisher mass bounce: every permanent matching `filter` across all battlefields returns to its owner's hand unless that permanent's OWNER pays `manaCost` — "unless that player pays" refers back to the owner, so a permanent that changed hands is paid for by the player whose hand would receive it, not the current controller. One independent may-pay decision per permanent, offered in APNAP order by owner (CR 101.4). Cut the Tethers (`PermanentHasSubtypePredicate(SPIRIT)`, `"{3}"`)

## Graveyard return

- `ReturnCardFromGraveyardEffect.builder().destination(HAND|BATTLEFIELD|TOP_OF_OWNERS_LIBRARY)...build()` — unified graveyard return (see EFFECTS_INDEX.md for full builder API). `.targetGraveyard(true).targetPutIntoGraveyardFromBattlefieldThisTurn(true)` = "target creature card in a graveyard that was put there from the battlefield this turn" (Grim Return)
- `ForgottenLoreEffect()` — SPELL, player-targeting spec (`harmful(PLAYER)`; pair with `PlayerPredicateTargetFilter(OPPONENT)`): the targeted opponent chooses a card in your graveyard, then you may pay {G} to repeat with every already-chosen card excluded; the last chosen card goes to your hand. Forgotten Lore. Alternates a mandatory `PendingInteraction.GraveyardChoice` (`MAY_ABILITY_TARGET`, cardPool = your graveyard minus chosen, completed via `graveyardTargetOperation.resolutionTimeForgottenLoreResume` — records only, moves nothing) with a `ChoiceContext.ForgottenLorePaymentChoice` list pick, both resuming over `GameData.forgottenLore` + `rerunCurrentEffectAfterInteraction`. The spell itself is excluded from the pool (CR 608.2n). No prompt when the controller can't afford {G}; loop ends when no unchosen card remains
- `RecordReturnedGraveyardCardManaValueEffect()` — Vengeful Rebirth glue: sets the entry's event value to the mana value of the card on `entry.targetId` (a graveyard target) **only when that nonland card was actually returned to the controller's hand** (in-hand check gates on the return; land / illegal-removed target records 0). `TargetSpec.NONE`, added UNBOUND after the `ReturnCardFromGraveyardEffect` so the resolver keeps `targetId` on the graveyard card. Pair with a bound `DealDamageToAnyTargetEffect(new EventValue(), false, false, 0)` on an any-target group to deal "damage equal to that card's mana value to any target" (0 = no damage)
- `ReturnSourceFromGraveyardAttachedToTargetEffect()` — GY activated: return source Aura attached to target creature (Gryff's Boon; wire `targetId`)
- `ReturnTriggeringLandFromGraveyardToBattlefieldEffect(UUID landCardId)` — Sacred Ground's trigger effect: return the identified land from the graveyard to the battlefield under its owner's control. Register the template with `null` on the `ON_ALLY_LAND_PUT_INTO_GRAVEYARD_BY_OPPONENT` slot; the collector stamps the real card id.
- `ReturnTargetCardOnDeathThisTurnEffect(boolean enterTapped)` — SPELL delayed trigger (Graceful Reprieve): if the targeted creature dies this turn, return that card to the battlefield under its owner's control. Pair with a creature `target(...)`. No-arg = untapped; pass `true` to return tapped (Supernatural Stamina, which also adds a `BoostTargetCreatureEffect(2, 0)` on the same target).
- `CreateTokenOnTargetDeathThisTurnEffect(CreateTokenEffect token)` — SPELL delayed trigger (Skeletonize): if the targeted creature dies this turn, the effect's controller creates `token`'s token(s). Pair with a creature `target(...)` and (typically) a `DealDamageToTargetCreatureEffect` in the same slot. Death pipeline pushes the token effect under the recorded controller.
- `ExileDyingCreatureCardAndCreateTokenEffect(CreateTokenEffect token)` — ON_DAMAGED_CREATURE_DIES effect (Unscythe, Killer of Kings), wrap in `MayEffect`: exile the dying creature's card from its owner's graveyard and, if the exile happens, create `token`. Implements `DyingCreatureCardAwareEffect` so the death-trigger collector binds the dying card id (the may flow drops the stack entry's triggering-card id). No exile if the card already left the graveyard (a token, or removed in response) → no token. On an Equipment this fires for the currently-equipped creature's damage (`GraveyardService.triggerDamagedCreatureDiesAbilities` scans Equipment attached to the damaging creature).
- `ReturnTriggeringCardFromGraveyardToBattlefieldEffect()` — triggered-ability effect the death pipeline pushes for the above; returns the stack entry's card from its owner's graveyard to the battlefield. Not added to a card directly.
- `ReturnOneOfEachSubtypeFromGraveyardToHandEffect(List<CardSubtype>)` — one of each subtype
- `PutTargetCardsFromGraveyardOnTopOfLibraryEffect(CardPredicate[, int maxTargets])` — graveyard to top of library; one-arg = any number, two-arg caps it (Reinforcements = 3). `fromOpponentGraveyard(filter, n)` targets a single opponent's graveyard instead and stacks the cards on that opponent's library (Misinformation = 3); `fromAnyPlayerGraveyard(filter, n)` allows any one player's graveyard, controller's included, and is usable from an activated ability via `Zone.GRAVEYARD` `targetIds` (Lodestone Bauble = 4 basic lands)
- `ReturnTargetCardsFromGraveyardToHandEffect(CardPredicate, int)` — up to N cards to hand
- `ReturnTargetCardsFromGraveyardToBattlefieldEffect(CardPredicate)` — exactly X target cards to the battlefield, with X taken from the spell's paid X value
- `ShuffleTargetCardsFromGraveyardIntoLibraryEffect(CardPredicate, int)` — target player shuffles N cards
- `ShuffleTargetCardsFromControllerGraveyardIntoLibraryEffect(CardPredicate, int)` — controller shuffles up to N target cards from their own graveyard into library (ETB: Ghostly Castigator; "up to" = you may)
- `ReturnDyingCreatureToBattlefieldAndAttachSourceEffect()` — reanimate + equip
- `ReturnDyingOpponentCreatureUnderYourControlEffect()` — ON_OPPONENT_CREATURE_DIES: steal the dying creature from its owner's graveyard onto your battlefield (Necroskitter); collector adds the "you may" and stamps the dying card id
- `GainLifeEqualToDyingCreatureToughnessEffect()` — ON_OPPONENT_CREATURE_DIES: you gain life equal to the dying creature's last-known effective toughness (Grim Feast). The collector snapshots the toughness from the trigger context and bakes it into a concrete `GainLifeEffect`
- `EnchantedCreatureDiesLoseLifeEqualPowerGainLifeEqualToughnessEffect()` — ON_ENCHANTED_PERMANENT_PUT_INTO_GRAVEYARD: controller loses life = LKI power, you gain life = LKI toughness (Death Watch). One stack entry; bakes into `EnchantedCreatureControllerLosesLifeEffect` + `GainLifeEffect`
- `StealDyingOpponentPermanentUnlessPaysLifeEffect(int lifeCost)` — ON_OPPONENT_PERMANENT_PUT_INTO_GRAVEYARD_FROM_BATTLEFIELD punisher (`ControlStealingEffect`): any-type opponent permanent dies; that opponent pays `lifeCost` life or you put that card onto the battlefield under your control (Prince of Thralls, 3). Can't-pay = auto-steal; collector stamps dying card id + paying opponent
- `PutCardFromOpponentGraveyardOntoBattlefieldEffect(boolean tapped, CardPredicate filter, boolean requireManaValueEqualsX)` — put target card matching `filter` from an opponent's graveyard onto battlefield under your control, correctly tracked as stolen (returns to owner on leaving). `(boolean tapped)` and `()` default to artifact-or-creature + MV==X + mill-X (Geth). Ashen Powder: `(false, new CardTypePredicate(CREATURE), false)` — creature only, any MV, no mill
- `LivingDeathEffect()` — SPELL slot, card-specific (Living Death). Each player exiles all creature cards from their graveyard, then every creature on every battlefield is sacrificed (`DestructionSupport.performSimultaneousSacrifice`), then each exiled card enters under the player whose graveyard it came from. Creatures sacrificed by step two are NOT reanimated; the returning cards enter as one CR 614.12 batch. `BoardWipeEffect`
- `UndyingReturnEffect()` — Undying (CR 702.93) resolution: return the dying card from its owner's graveyard to the battlefield with a +1/+1 counter. Do NOT add to a card directly; it is pushed automatically by `PermanentRemovalService` when a creature with the `UNDYING` keyword dies with no +1/+1 counters. The keyword is loaded from Scryfall.
- `PersistReturnEffect()` — Persist (CR 702.79) resolution: return the dying card from its owner's graveyard to the battlefield with a -1/-1 counter. Do NOT add to a card directly; it is pushed automatically by `PermanentRemovalService` when a creature with the `PERSIST` keyword dies with no -1/-1 counters. The keyword is loaded from Scryfall.
- `PutCreatureFromOpponentGraveyardOntoBattlefieldWithExileEffect()` — opponent's creature with exile
- `GrantTargetCreatureCardGraveyardCastAndCopyActivatedAbilitiesEffect()` — target creature card in any graveyard may be cast this turn; when cast, source gains its activated abilities
- `GrantSourceActivatedAbilitiesUntilEndOfTurnEffect(List<ActivatedAbility>, String)` — delayed source grant used after casting the selected graveyard creature

## Draw / discard / hand manipulation

- `DrawCardUnlessTargetSacrificesCreatureOrPaysLifeEffect(int lifeCost)` — targets an opponent; that opponent chooses to sacrifice a creature, pay `lifeCost` life, or let the effect's controller draw a card. Options are pruned when the sacrifice or payment is impossible; a single remaining draw option resolves automatically. Indulgent Tormentor (M15)
- `PutControllerCardFromHandOnTopOfLibraryEffect()` — controller must choose one card from a nonempty hand and put it on top of their library; empty hand resolves with no prompt and reuses the top-only `PutCardsFromHandOnLibraryCardChoice` interaction

- `DrawCardEffect(DynamicAmount)` or `(int)` — controller draws that many; use `XValue` for "draw X", `PermanentCount`/`CardsInGraveyard`/`CountersOnSource` for "draw a card for each …"
- `DrawCardRevealDiscardUnlessLandEffect()` — controller draws a card and reveals it; if it isn't a land card, it is discarded (not opponent-caused; fires discard triggers). Card-filtering: only lands kept (Sindbad, `{T}:`). `CardDrawingEffect` (drawnCardAmount = 1)
- `DrawCardPerChosenTypeCountEffect()` — "Choose a creature type. Draw a card for each permanent you control of that type" (Distant Melody). Resolution-time creature-type choice (`beginSpellCreatureTypeChoice`, stored on `GameData.chosenSpellSubtype`), Changeling-aware count; draw sibling of `DealDamageToTargetCreatureEqualToChosenTypeCountEffect`
- `DestroyAllCreaturesOfChosenTypeEffect()` — "Destroy all creatures of the creature type of your choice" (Extinction). Resolution-time creature-type choice (`beginSpellCreatureTypeChoice`, stored on `GameData.chosenSpellSubtype`), then a Changeling-aware wipe of that type across every battlefield; `BoardWipeEffect`. For a fixed type use `DestroyAllPermanentsEffect(PermanentHasSubtypePredicate(...))`
- `GainLifePerChosenTypeCountEffect(int lifePerPermanent)` — "Choose a creature type. You gain `lifePerPermanent` life for each permanent you control of that type" (Luminescent Rain = `(2)`). Resolution-time creature-type choice (`beginSpellCreatureTypeChoice`, stored on `GameData.chosenSpellSubtype`), Changeling-aware count; gains `count*lifePerPermanent` life. Life sibling of `DrawCardPerChosenTypeCountEffect`
- `BoostTargetCreaturePerChosenTypeCountEffect(int powerPer, int toughnessPer)` — "Choose a creature type. Target creature gets `powerPer`/`toughnessPer` until end of turn for each permanent of the chosen type you control" (Pack's Disdain = `(-1, -1)`). Resolution-time creature-type choice (`beginSpellCreatureTypeChoice`, stored on `GameData.chosenSpellSubtype`), Changeling-aware count; applies `count*powerPer`/`count*toughnessPer` as a until-end-of-turn P/T modifier. Boost sibling of `DealDamageToTargetCreatureEqualToChosenTypeCountEffect`; pair with a `PermanentIsCreaturePredicate` target filter
- `PayXLifeDrawXCardsEffect()` — SPELL: resolution-time X choice — controller picks X (capped at current life), pays X life, draws X cards (Necrologia). Pair with `setSpellCastTimingRestriction(YOUR_END_STEP)` for "cast only during your end step"
- `EachPlayerDrawsCardEffect(DynamicAmount)` or `(int)` — each player (turn order) draws that many; the amount is re-evaluated per drawing player, so player-relative amounts (`CardsInGraveyard(..., CONTROLLER)` = each player's own graveyard, Nature's Resurgence) count that player's objects. `XValue()` for "each player draws X" (Prosperity), `int` for a fixed count
- `DrawCardForTargetPlayerEffect(DynamicAmount, boolean requiresUntapped, boolean targets)` or `(int)` — target/entry player draws; `XValue` for "target player draws X"; draws for every target when the entry is multi-target
- `TargetPermanentControllerDrawsCardEffect()` — the controller of the permanent targeted by this stack entry draws a card (permanent analogue of `TargetSpellControllerDrawsCardEffect`). Piggybacks on `entry.getTargetId()`, so pair it with a sibling targeting effect that owns the permanent target (e.g. `PutCounterOnTargetPermanentEffect`); it contributes no target of its own. Gwafa Hazid, Profiteer ("Put a bribery counter on target creature you don't control. Its controller draws a card.")
- `DyingCreatureControllerMayDrawCardEffect()` — ON_ANY_CREATURE_DIES marker: whenever any creature dies, the DYING creature's controller (may be an opponent of the source) may draw a card (Fecundity). Unlike a plain `MayEffect(DrawCardEffect())` on that slot, which offers the draw to the source's controller
- `DefendingPlayerMayDrawCardEffect()` — ON_ATTACK marker: "whenever this creature attacks, defending player may draw a card" (Sibilant Spirit). `CombatAttackService` routes the optional draw to the defending player (or the attacked planeswalker's controller), not the attacking creature's controller. Unlike a plain `MayEffect(DrawCardEffect())`, which offers the draw to the source's controller
- `TargetOpponentMayDrawCardEffect()` — "Target opponent may draw a card" (Soldevi Heretic). Declares `TargetSpec.NONE` — the opponent is derived (two-player engine), so the entry's target slot stays free for a sibling targeting effect. Its handler queues a `MayEffect(DrawCardEffect())` for that opponent, so the "may" is theirs; a plain `MayEffect(DrawCardEffect())` would offer it to the source's controller
- `TargetOpponentCreatesTokenEffect(CreateTokenEffect token)` — "Target opponent creates [token]" (Phelddagrif); the token is created under the opponent's control. Declares `TargetSpec.NONE` (opponent derived, target slot stays free). Deliberately not a `TokenCreatingEffect` — it is a drawback, not AI value
- `DyingCreatureControllerDiscardsCardEffect()` — ON_ANY_CREATURE_DIES marker: whenever a qualifying creature dies, the DYING creature's controller (may be an opponent) discards a card (mandatory). Bereavement wraps it in `TriggeringCardConditionalEffect(CardColorPredicate(GREEN), …)` for "a green creature dies"
- `DyingCreatureControllerSacrificesPermanentsEffect(int count, PermanentPredicate filter)` — ON_ANY_CREATURE_DIES marker: whenever a qualifying creature dies, the DYING creature's controller (may be an opponent) sacrifices `count` matching permanent(s) of their choice (mandatory). Earthlink uses `(1, new PermanentIsLandPredicate())`
- Loot ("draw N, then discard M") — _no dedicated effect_: compose `DrawCardEffect(N)` + `DiscardEffect(M, DiscardRecipient.CONTROLLER)` (flat pair on a spell/ability effect list — one shared entry, so the draw resolves before the discard). On a single-effect wrapper or trigger slot, bundle as `SequenceEffect.of(new DrawCardEffect(N), new DiscardEffect(M, DiscardRecipient.CONTROLLER))`, e.g. `MayEffect(SequenceEffect.of(...), prompt)` for "you may draw a card. If you do, discard a card." Once you've drawn, the discard is unconditional — unlike rummage below, whose draw is contingent on an actual discard
- `DiscardAndDrawCardEffect(int discard, int draw)` — rummage
- `DiscardCardAndBoostSelfEffect(int power, int toughness)` — discard a card, then source gets +power/+toughness until end of turn. Wrap in `MayEffect` for "you may discard a card. If you do, this creature gets +X/+Y until end of turn" (Furyblade Vampire). Uses `DiscardFollowUp.boost`; empty hand → no boost
- `DiscardCardThenEffect(CardPredicate, CardEffect, String)` — discard one matching hand card; if you do, push thenEffect as a reflexive trigger. Pack Guardian = `MayEffect(DiscardCardThenEffect(CardTypePredicate(LAND), CreateTokenEffect(Wolf 2/2), "a land card"))`. Uses `DiscardFollowUp.thenEffect`
- `DrawDiscardBoostSelfByDiscardedManaValueEffect()` — draw 1, discard 1, then source gets +X/+0 until end of turn where X = the discarded card's mana value (Spellbound Dragon, `ON_ATTACK`). A pending hook (`pendingBoostSourceByDiscardedManaValue`) applies the boost after the interactive discard. Compare `DrawDiscardTransformIfCreatureDiscardedEffect` (same draw-discard-then-conditional shape, transform instead of boost)
- `DiscardEffect(DynamicAmount, DiscardRecipient, boolean random)` — the whole discard family; `recipient` dz??? {`CONTROLLER`, `TARGET_PLAYER`, `TARGET_PERMANENT_CONTROLLER`, `TARGET_PLAYER_OR_PERMANENT_CONTROLLER`, `DEFENDING_PLAYER`, `EACH_PLAYER`, `EACH_OPPONENT`}, `random` picks chosen vs random discard. `DEFENDING_PLAYER` declares no target and reads the combat trigger's `attackedTargetId` (attacked player, or attacked planeswalker's controller) — "whenever this creature becomes blocked/attacks, defending player discards a card" (Slate Street Ruffian). `TARGET_PLAYER_OR_PERMANENT_CONTROLLER` (Blightning) declares no target and piggybacks on a companion player-or-planeswalker effect's `targetId`, routing the discard to the targeted player or the targeted planeswalker's controller. `(int, recipient, random)` / `(DynamicAmount, recipient)` / `(int, recipient)` convenience ctors (last two non-random). `CountersOnSource(CHARGE)` for per-charge-counter (Shrine of Limitless Power), `XValue()` for Mind Shatter (`TARGET_PLAYER`, random)
- `RevealHandAndRandomDiscardCardTypeEffect(CardType)` — target player reveals hand, discards one card of type at random (Rag Man, CREATURE); player-targeting spec
- `TempestEfreetAnteExchangeEffect(int lifeCost)` — ante punisher (`benign(PLAYER)`, pair with a "target opponent" `PlayerPredicateTargetFilter`): target opponent may pay `lifeCost` life; if not, they reveal a random card that goes to the ability controller's hand and the sacrificed source goes to the opponent's graveyard. Payable opponents get a may-ability prompt (accept/decline in `mayfx/TempestEfreetAnteExchangeHandler`), unpayable ones exchange at once. Single-game zone movements only — no runtime ownership change (Tempest Efreet)
- `TimmerianFiendsAnteExchangeEffect()` — ante punisher (`harmful(PERMANENT, PermanentIsArtifactPredicate)`, pair with an artifact `PermanentPredicateTargetFilter`): the targeted artifact's owner may ante the top card of their library; if not, the artifact goes to the ability controller's graveyard and the sacrificed source to that owner's graveyard. Prompted via may-ability (accept/decline in `mayfx/TimmerianFiendsAnteExchangeHandler`); an empty library exchanges at once. Single-game zone movements only — no runtime ownership change (Timmerian Fiends)
- `BronzeTabletAnteExchangeEffect(int lifeCost)` — ante trade (`harmful(PERMANENT)`, pair with a "nontoken permanent an opponent owns" `PermanentPredicateTargetFilter`): exiles both the source and the targeted permanent, then the permanent's owner may pay `lifeCost` life; if they do, the source goes from exile to its owner's graveyard; if not, both stay exiled (ante swap). Payable owners get a may-ability prompt (accept/decline in `mayfx/BronzeTabletAnteExchangeHandler`), unpayable ones swap at once. Single-game zone movements only — no runtime ownership change (Bronze Tablet)
- `RebirthAnteEffect()` — non-targeting; "Each player may ante the top card of their library. If a player does, that player's life total becomes 20." `normalfx` seeds one accept/decline `PendingMayAbility` per player (APNAP, active first; empty-library players skipped); `mayfx/RebirthAnteHandler` antes the accepting player's top card (removed from the game → exile, like the Tempest Efreet ante convention) and sets their life to 20 via `applySetLifeTotal`. Rebirth
- `AmuletOfQuozAnteEffect()` — ante punisher (`harmful(PLAYER)`, pair with a "target opponent" `PlayerPredicateTargetFilter`): target opponent may ante their top library card; if they don't, the controller flips a coin — win means the opponent loses the game, loss means the controller does. `normalfx` prompts the opponent (empty library ⇒ flip at once, no prompt), `mayfx/AmuletOfQuozAnteHandler` antes on accept (card → exile) or flips on decline. Losses go through the `resolveLoss` gate (Amulet of Quoz)
- `RevealTargetHandEffect()` — target player reveals their whole hand (logged); nothing further happens. Pair with a companion targeted effect on the same player, e.g. `LoseLifeEffect(1, TARGET_PLAYER)` (Thoughtcutter Agent). `TargetSpec.benign(PLAYER)`
- `ExileTargetPlayerHandEffect()` — exile target player's whole hand (no choice, no play permission; not a discard, so no discard triggers). `TargetSpec.harmful(PLAYER)`. Pair on the same SPELL slot with `ExileGraveyardCardsEffect(TARGET_PLAYER_ENTIRE)` for "exile all cards from target player's hand and graveyard" (Identity Crisis); both PLAYER specs collapse to one target
- `ExileAllHandsEffect()` — non-targeting all-players counterpart: exile every card in every player's hand. Pair on the same SPELL slot with `ExileGraveyardCardsEffect(ALL_PLAYERS)` for "exile all cards from all hands and graveyards" (Worldfire)
- `DiscardHandEffect(DiscardRecipient)` — discard entire hand(s); no-arg = controller. `TARGET_PLAYER` implements `CombatDamageTriggerContextEffect` (DAMAGED_PLAYER), so it works as an ON_DAMAGE_TO_PLAYER / ON_COMBAT_DAMAGE_TO_PLAYER trigger with the damaged player baked in (Nicol Bolas)
- `DiscardEffect(DynamicAmount, DiscardRecipient, boolean random)` — the whole discard family; `recipient` dz??? {`CONTROLLER`, `TARGET_PLAYER`, `EACH_PLAYER`, `EACH_OPPONENT`}, `random` picks chosen vs random discard. `(int, recipient, random)` / `(DynamicAmount, recipient)` / `(int, recipient)` convenience ctors (last two non-random). `CountersOnSource(CHARGE)` for per-charge-counter (Shrine of Limitless Power), `XValue()` for Mind Shatter (`TARGET_PLAYER`, random)
- `RevealHandAndRandomDiscardCardTypeEffect(CardType)` — target player reveals hand, discards one card of type at random (Rag Man, CREATURE); `canTargetPlayer`
- `DiscardOwnHandThenEachOpponentSacrificesCreaturePerCardEffect()` — discard controller's hand, then each opponent sacrifices a creature of their choice for each card discarded this way. Sacrifice count = cards discarded (snapshotted before the discard); empty hand ⇒ nothing. Delegates to the discard-hand + forced-sacrifice (EACH_OPPONENT) handlers. Malfegor (ETB)
- `DiscardHandEffect(DiscardRecipient)` — discard entire hand(s); no-arg = controller
- `EachPlayerDiscardsFractionOfHandRoundedUpEffect(int divisor)` — each player discards `ceil(handSize/divisor)` cards of their choice, recomputed per player (APNAP). Use (not `DiscardEffect(EACH_PLAYER)`, which applies one shared amount) when the count is a per-player fraction. Pox (3)
- `EachPlayerSacrificesFractionRoundedUpEffect(int divisor, PermanentPredicate filter)` — each player sacrifices `ceil(matching/divisor)` of their matching permanents of their choice, recomputed per player (APNAP simultaneous, CR 101.4). Pox: `(3, new PermanentIsCreaturePredicate())` + `(3, new PermanentIsLandPredicate())`
- `EachPlayerSacrificesDownToFewestEffect(PermanentPredicate filter)` — each player keeps a number of their matching permanents equal to the fewest any player controls and sacrifices the rest, of their own choice (APNAP simultaneous, CR 101.4; min=0 → everyone sacrifices all, no prompt). Balance: `(new PermanentIsLandPredicate())` + `(new PermanentIsCreaturePredicate())`
- `EachPlayerExilesPermanentsOrCardsFromHandEffect(DynamicAmount amount)` — each player exiles `amount` objects chosen from their own battlefield **and/or** hand — the only mixed-zone selection in the engine (`PendingInteraction.ExilePermanentsOrHandCardsChoice`, card ids for both zones). Prompts run APNAP but nothing is exiled until every player has answered (CR 101.4); fewer objects than `amount` ⇒ all of them, no prompt. Descent into Madness: `new CountersOnSource(CounterType.DESPAIR)` as the second step of a `SequenceEffect` after `PutCountersOnSelfEffect(CounterType.DESPAIR)`
- `EachPlayerDiscardsDownToFewestEffect()` — each player discards down to the smallest hand size, of their own choice (APNAP). Balance's discard step
- `NaturalBalanceEffect()` — land counts are read once, up front: players with 6+ lands choose which lands to sacrifice down to 5, players with 4 or fewer may search their library for up to `5 - lands` basic land cards onto the battlefield (untapped), then shuffle. Both queues are APNAP-ordered and disjoint; the engine runs the searches first and the sacrifices after (`NaturalBalanceSupport`, carried on `LibrarySearchFollowUp.NaturalBalanceQueue`). Natural Balance
- `DiscardHandUnlessPaysLifeEffect(int lifeCost)` — target player discards their entire hand unless they pay `lifeCost` life; target chooses (can't-pay → auto-discard). Pair with `PlayerPredicateTargetFilter`. Tyrannize (7)
- `RevealHandDiscardMatchingCardsUnlessPaysLifeEffect(CardPredicate cardFilter, int lifeCost)` — target player reveals their hand; for each revealed card matching `cardFilter`, that player discards it unless they pay `lifeCost` life. One independent prompt per matching card (offered sequentially); can't-pay → auto-discard. Pair with `PlayerPredicateTargetFilter`. Sirocco (blue instant cards, 4)
- `DiscardOwnHandThenDrawThatManyEffect()` — discard entire hand, then draw that many
- `TargetPlayerDiscardsHandThenDrawsThatManyEffect()` — target player discards entire hand, then draws that many; player-targeting spec. Collective Defiance
- `TargetPlayerDiscardsThenDrawsThatManyEffect(N)` — target player discards N cards, then draws as many as they discarded (draw = `min(N, hand size)`); player-targeting spec. Forget
- `DiscardThenReturnFromGraveyardToHandEffect(DynamicAmount)` — controller discards `amount` cards, then returns a card from their graveyard to hand for each card discarded this way (returns `min(amount, hand size)`, chosen one at a time). Recall = `XValue()`; pair with a trailing `ExileSpellEffect()`
- `DiscardOwnHandThenDrawEqualToTargetPlayerHandSizeEffect()` — discard entire hand, then draw equal to target player's hand size (counted at draw time)
- `DiscardUnlessReturnLandToHandEffect()` — "discard a card unless you return a land you control to its owner's hand." May-ability escape: accept returns a chosen land (shared `BounceCreature` context, to owner's hand), decline discards a card; no lands ⇒ mandatory discard. Return-a-land sibling of `DiscardUnlessExileCardFromGraveyardEffect`. Pair with a leading `DrawCardEffect(N)` (Tragic Lesson = `DrawCardEffect(2)` + this)
- `DiscardOwnHandThenDrawEffect(DynamicAmount)` — discard entire hand, then draw equal to a DynamicAmount (evaluated at draw time; independent of discard count). Knollspine Dragon = `DamageDealtToTargetPlayerThisTurn`
- `EachPlayerDiscardsHandThenDrawsThatManyEffect()` — each player (APNAP) discards their entire hand, then draws that many
- `EachPlayerDiscardsHandThenDrawsGreatestDiscardedEffect()` — each player discards their hand, then all draw equal to the greatest number discarded (Windfall)
- `EachPlayerDiscardsAnyNumberThenDrawsThatManyEffect()` — each player (APNAP) discards any number of cards (their choice), then draws that many (Flux)
- `EachPlayerMayDiscardUpToThenTakeDamageEffect(int amount)` — each player (APNAP) may discard up to `amount` cards (their choice, capped at hand size), then is dealt `amount - discarded` damage; empty/zero-discard players take the full `amount` (Mind Bomb, `amount` = 3)
- `DestroyTokensCreatedWithSourceEffect(boolean cannotBeRegenerated)` — destroy every surviving token created with the source permanent (tracked in `GameData.sourceCreatedTokens`); END_STEP_TRIGGERED / ON_SELF_LEAVES_BATTLEFIELD (Tombstone Stairwell)
- `EachPlayerCreatesTokenEffect(CreateTokenEffect token)` / `(token, boolean recordAsCreatedWithSource)` — each player (turn order) creates the wrapped `token` under their own control; the token's dynamic amount is re-evaluated per creating player, so `CountScope.CONTROLLER` reads each player's own board (Waiting in the Weeds)
- `EachPlayerPaysAnyLifeForTokensEffect(CreateTokenEffect token)` — starting with controller, each player may pay any amount of life, round-robin until a full round of no payments; each creates one `token` per life paid (Plague of Vermin)
- `ExileTopCardsMayPlayUntilNextTurnEffect(DynamicAmount count)` or `(int count)` — exile top N from library, may play until end of your next turn (owner-relative expiry via `ExileSupport.grantPlayUntilOwnersNextTurn`). Use `EventValue()` for "equal to the excess damage dealt this way" (Archaic's Agony)
- `ExileTopCardMayPlayUntilNextUpkeepEffect()` — exile the top card of **your** library; may play it (any type) until the beginning of your next upkeep, then it just loses play permission and stays in exile (Elkin Bottle). Grants `exilePlayPermissions` + queues a `RevokeExilePlayPermissionAtNextUpkeep` delayed action (drained in `StepTriggerService.handleUpkeepTriggers` when the controller is active). Unlike `ExileTopCardsMayPlayUntilNextTurnEffect` the window ends one turn earlier (upkeep, not turn-end); unlike Grinning Totem the card is never moved to a graveyard
- `ExileTopCardsFaceDownMayPlayUntilNextUpkeepEffect(int count)` — exile top N of **your** library **face down**; may look at / play them until the beginning of your next upkeep; unplayed cards go to owner's graveyard then (Three Wishes). Grants `exilePlayPermissions` + queues `ExileToOwnerGraveyardAtNextUpkeep` per card (same cleanup as Grinning Totem / `expiresAtNextUpkeep`)
- `PutTopCardOfGraveyardOnBottomOfLibraryEffect()` — put the top (most recently added) card of **your** graveyard on the bottom of your library; no-op if the graveyard is empty (Soldevi Digger, `{2}`)
- `PutTopCreatureFromTargetGraveyardOnLibraryTopEffect()` — if the top (most recently added) card of **target player's** graveyard is a creature, put it on top of that player's library; no-op if empty or non-creature (Guiding Spirit, `{T}`; benign PLAYER target)
- `ExileTopCardOfOpponentLibraryControllerMayPlayThisTurnEffect()` — target opponent exiles the top card of their library; the source's **controller** may play that card (lands and spells, normal costs/timing) until **end of turn** (Knacksaw Clique). Card owned by the opponent; grants `exilePlayPermissions` to the controller + `exilePlayPermissionsExpireEndOfTurn`. Two-player: single opponent derived
- `ExileTopUntilNonlandOfTargetOpponentMayCastThisTurnEffect()` — target opponent digs until a nonland; controller may **cast** that card without paying until end of turn (Nicol Bolas, God-Pharaoh +2). Lands stay exiled with no permission; free cast via `exilePlayWithoutPayingManaCost` + EOT expiry
- `EachOpponentExilesFromHandEffect(N)` — each opponent exiles N cards from hand (their choice), non-targeting, APNAP (Nicol Bolas, God-Pharaoh +1). Fewer than N ⇒ whole hand. Reuses `EXILE_FROM_HAND_CHOICE` with a remaining-choosers queue
- `ExileTopCardMayPlayThisTurnEffect(boolean withoutPayingManaCost)` / `(int count, boolean withoutPayingManaCost)` — exile the top card (or top `count` cards, Act on Impulse = 3) of **your** library; may play them (any type, lands included) until **end of turn**. `false` = normal costs/timing; `true` = cast it without paying its mana cost. Grants `exilePlayPermissions` + `exilePlayPermissionsExpireEndOfTurn` (+ `exilePlayWithoutPayingManaCost` for the free variant, consumed in `SpellCastingService.playCardFromExile` and cleared at cleanup). Oracle's Vault (`{2},{T}` = `false` + `PutCountersOnSelfEffect(BRICK)`; `{T}` = `true`, gated on 3 brick counters). Unlike `ExileTopCardMayCastNonlandThisTurnEffect` (nonland only), lands also get permission
- `ExileRandomCardFromHandMayPlayThisTurnEffect()` — target/active player exiles a **random** hand card; may play it this turn (EOT permission); unplayed → owner's graveyard at the next end step via `ExileToOwnerGraveyardAtNextEndStep` (Elkin Lair, `EACH_UPKEEP_TRIGGERED`)
- `ExileTopCardsMayCastMatchingThisTurnEffect(int count, CardPredicate filter)` — exile top N; grant end-of-turn cast permission only for cards matching `filter` (Chandra, Dressed to Kill: count 1/5 + `CardColorPredicate(RED)`)
- `DealDamageEqualToManaSpentToCastToAnyTargetEffect(CardPredicate)` — whenever you cast a matching spell, deal damage equal to mana spent to cast it to any target (`ON_CONTROLLER_CASTS_SPELL` or emblem staticEffects; Chandra Dressed to Kill emblem)
- `CreateEmblemEffect(List<CardEffect> staticEffects, String reminderText[, EmblemRecipient recipient])` — **the** effect for every "you get an emblem with …" ultimate. Declare the emblem's contents on the card: the handler stores `staticEffects` verbatim on the `Emblem` and logs `<player> gets an emblem with "<reminderText>".` (pass the text unquoted). `recipient` defaults to `CONTROLLER`; `TARGET_PLAYER` gives the emblem to the targeted player and makes the effect declare a benign `PLAYER` target — pair with `PlayerPredicateTargetFilter(OPPONENT)` (Garruk, Apex Predator −8). Emblems are never removed
- `ExileTargetSpellUntilSourceLeavesEffect()` — ON_ENTER_BATTLEFIELD "exile target spell" that records the exiling permanent on the exiled card (Spell Queller). Not countering, so "can't be countered" does not stop it; put the mana-value limit on the card's `StackEntryPredicateTargetFilter`. Pair with `MayCastCardsExiledWithSourceEffect()`
- `MayCastCardsExiledWithSourceEffect()` — ON_SELF_LEAVES_BATTLEFIELD "the exiled card's owner may cast that card without paying its mana cost" (Spell Queller). The collector bakes the leaving permanent's id in; each card exiled with that source is offered to its **owner** via `MayPlayExiledCardWithoutPayingManaCostEffect`. Declining leaves it exiled
- `ExileTargetSpellWithDelayCountersEffect()` — "target spell's controller exiles it with X delay counters on it" (Ertai's Meddling). Not countering, so "can't be countered" does not stop it. Tracked in `GameData.delayedSpellExiles`
- `RemoveDelayCounterFromExiledSpellEffect(UUID cardId)` — the paired upkeep trigger; on the last delay counter the card leaves exile and is put back onto the stack (not cast) with its original X and targets
- `ExileTargetPermanentMayPlayUntilNextTurnEffect()` — exile the target permanent, its owner may play it until end of their next turn (e.g. Suspend Aggression; pair with a permanent target filter). Tokens exiled this way cease to exist
- `ExileUpToOneCardFromGraveyardEffect()` — "you may exile target card from a graveyard" as a non-targeting, resolution-time, cross-graveyard "up to one" select (choose zero = the optional "you may"). Rides any resolution path — `ON_DEATH` and a folded cycling ability `[this, DrawCardEffect(1)]` (Grixis Sojourners). Completed in `GraveyardChoiceHandlerService.handleMultipleCardsChosen` via `graveyardTargetOperation.resolutionTimeExileResume`: exiles the chosen card, then resumes remaining effects (e.g. the cycling draw). Graveyard analogue of `ExileUpToNAttackingCreaturesEffect`
- `ExileCardsFromGraveyardEffect(int maxTargets, int lifeGain[, boolean lifeGainPerExiledCard, CardPredicate filter, boolean assignNoCombatDamage])` — exile up to N target graveyard cards (chosen as the spell/ability goes on the stack; zero = the "you may") and gain life. `lifeGainPerExiledCard` makes `lifeGain` per card actually exiled, `filter` restricts which cards are choosable, `assignNoCombatDamage` adds the entry's `sourcePermanentId` to `creaturesPreventedFromDealingCombatDamage` when at least one card was exiled. Rooftop Percher `(2, 3)`, Scarab Feast `(3, 0)`, Rysorian Badger `(2, 1, true, CardTypePredicate(CREATURE), true)` on `ON_ATTACKS_UNBLOCKED`. Implements `GraveyardCardChoosingEffect`
- `ExileTargetCardFromGraveyardMayPlayUntilNextTurnEffect(CardPredicate filter, boolean ownGraveyardOnly)` — exile a targeted graveyard card matching the filter, controller may play it until end of their next turn (e.g. Practiced Scrollsmith; ETB graveyard-target flow via `MultiGraveyardChoice`)
- `ExileTargetInstantOrSorceryFromOpponentGraveyardMayCastEffect()` — exile a targeted instant/sorcery from an opponent's graveyard; controller may cast it **this turn**, spending mana of any type, and it is exiled instead of going to a graveyard (Nita, Forum Conciliator). Uses `exilePlayPermissions` + `exilePlayPermissionsExpireEndOfTurn` + `exilePlayAnyManaType` + `exileInsteadOfGraveyard`. Targets graveyard (per-instance spec: any graveyard unless `ownGraveyardOnly`)
- `ExileGraveyardInstantsOrSorceriesAndCastCopiesEffect()` — exile **every** targeted instant/sorcery card from its graveyard, copy each, and cast the copies without paying their mana costs (Spelltwine). Leave the effect **unbound** (plain `addEffect`) so it reads the whole target list, and declare one `target(new GraveyardCardPredicateTargetFilter(instantOrSorcery, scope))` group per graveyard. Copies are queued through `ExileFreeCastQueueSupport.queueCopiesForFreeCast`, so a copy that needs a target pauses for the choice and the rest are still cast; each is cast as a copy and ceases to exist on resolution. Pair with `ExileSpellEffect()` for the "Exile Spelltwine" rider
- `SpendAnyManaTypeToCastEffect(Set<CardType> spellTypes)` — `EffectSlot.STATIC` marker: "You can spend mana of any type to cast [types] spells." Colored requirements of a matching spell (hand or library top) may be paid with mana of any type (Vizier of the Menagerie, CREATURE). Read via the `AnyManaTypeCastEffect` capability interface in `CastingPermissionService.canSpendAnyManaTypeToCast`
- `PlayTargetCardFromGraveyardWithoutPayingManaCostEffect(CardPredicate filter)` — "you may play target [filter] card from your **own** graveyard without paying its mana cost" (Horde of Notions). On resolution offers a may-play: land → battlefield, else cast for free. Targets the controller's own graveyard (kept `@ValidatesTarget` validator enforces own-only); routed by `MayCastHandlerService.handlePlayFromGraveyardChoice`
- `PlayImprintedCardWithoutPayingManaCostEffect()` — Hideaway "you may play the exiled card without paying its mana cost" activated ability (Howltooth Hollow). Offers a may-play of the source permanent's imprinted (face-down exiled) card: land → battlefield (counts as the land play for the turn), else cast from exile for free. Routed by `MayCastHandlerService.handlePlayImprintedCardChoice`. Gate with `ConditionalEffect(<play condition>, …)` (e.g. `NoPlayerHasCardsInHand`); pair with `ImprintFromTopCardsEffect(N)` on ON_ENTER_BATTLEFIELD
- `ChooseCardsFromTargetHandEffect(int|DynamicAmount count, List<CardType> excludedTypes[, List<CardType> includedTypes], HandChoiceDestination destination[, boolean returnOnSourceLeave])` — reveal target's hand, caster chooses N card(s) → `DISCARD` / `EXILE` / `TOP_OF_LIBRARY` (Duress, Kitesail Freebooter, Agonizing Memories). `count` accepts an `XValue()` for "choose X cards" (Mind Warp). Overload `(int count, List<CardType> excludedTypes, CardPredicate filter, HandChoiceDestination destination)` further narrows choosable cards by a `CardPredicate` — e.g. "nonlegendary" via `CardNotPredicate(CardSupertypePredicate(LEGENDARY))` (Lay Bare the Heart, on top of `excludedTypes=[LAND]`)
- `ChooseTwoFilteredCardsFromTargetHandToDiscardEffect(CardPredicate first, CardPredicate second)` — reveal hand; choose one matching each predicate (skip empty bands); discard those (Distended Mindbender). Second pick via `RevealedHandChoice.followUpFilter`
- `RevealCardsChooseOneToDiscardEffect(PermanentPredicate countFilter)` — target reveals X cards **of their choice** (X = number of the caster's permanents matching `countFilter`), then the caster picks one for the target to discard (Thieving Sprite, `PermanentHasAnySubtypePredicate(FAERIE)`). Unlike `ChooseCardsFromTargetHandEffect` the rest of the hand stays hidden; uses shared `RevealCardsDiscardChoice` (same path as Blackmail), phase 1 skipped when the hand is already ≤ X
- `PayAnyAmountOfLifeRevealAndExileEffect()` — "Pay any amount of life. Target opponent reveals that many cards from their hand. You choose one of them and exile it." (Vizkopa Confessor). Prompts the controller for 0..their life via `XValueChoice`, pays it, then runs the shared `RevealCardsDiscardChoice` flow with an `EXILE` destination; paying 0 reveals and exiles nothing. Targets an opponent — pair with `PlayerPredicateTargetFilter(OPPONENT)`
- `TargetRevealsCardsControllerChoosesDiscardEffect(int revealCount[, int discardCount])` — target player reveals `revealCount` cards **of their choice** from hand (whole hand if fewer); the controller sees only those and picks `discardCount` of them (default 1; fewer if the hand held fewer) for the target to discard (Blackmail = reveal 3/discard 1; Noggin Whack = reveal 3/discard 2). Two-stage `RevealCardsDiscardChoice` interaction, discard picks looped one at a time; player-targeting spec. Contrast `ChooseCardsFromTargetHandEffect` (whole hand revealed, controller chooses)
- `RevealTargetHandDrawPerMatchingCardEffect(List<CardSubtype> subtypes, List<CardColor> colors)` — target opponent reveals hand; draw one card per card matching any subtype/color (counted once). Pair with `target(PlayerPredicateTargetFilter(OPPONENT))` (Baleful Stare)
- `RevealHandChooseCreatureGainLifeDiscardEffect(List<CardColor> colors)` — target opponent reveals hand; caster chooses one creature card whose colors include any of `colors` (empty = any color), gains life equal to its toughness, then the target discards it. Pair with `target(PlayerPredicateTargetFilter(OPPONENT))`; player-targeting spec (Talara's Bane = `List.of(GREEN, WHITE)`)
- `DiscardAllCardsOfChosenColorEffect()` — caster chooses a color, target player discards all cards of that color from hand. Pair with `target(PlayerPredicateTargetFilter(ANY))` (Persecute)
- `LookAtHandEffect()` — look at hand
- `LookAtHandChooseNonlandToBottomAndDrawEffect()` — look at target player's hand; caster **may** choose a nonland card (optional decline); if chosen, target reveals it, bottoms it, then draws a card. Pair with `target(PlayerPredicateTargetFilter(ANY))` (Vendilion Clique, `ON_ENTER_BATTLEFIELD`)
- `ShuffleHandIntoLibraryAndDrawEffect()` — wheel
- `PutHandOnBottomOfLibraryAndDrawEffect()` — target player puts hand on bottom of library, draws that many (Teferi's Puzzle Box, `EACH_DRAW_TRIGGERED`)
- `SylvanLibraryDrawEffect()` — controller draws two additional cards, then must resolve up to two cards drawn this turn (still in hand): put each on top of library or pay 4 life (Sylvan Library; wrap in `MayEffect` in `DRAW_TRIGGERED`). Reads `GameData.cardsDrawnThisTurnIds`; prompts `PendingInteraction.SylvanLibraryChoice`
- `DrawThenPutCardsFromHandOnTopOrBottomOfLibraryEffect(int drawCount, int putCount[, HandToLibraryPlacement placement])` — draw `drawCount`, then choose `putCount` hand cards (multi-select) and put them back on your library. `PLAYER_CHOICE` (default) puts them **all** on top or **all** on the bottom (single pick applied to every chosen card, Dream Cache `(3, 2)`); `TOP` (Brainstorm) and `BOTTOM` (Amass the Components `(3, 1, BOTTOM)`) skip the destination prompt. Two chained interactions (`PutCardsFromHandOnLibraryCardChoice` → `PutCardsFromHandOnLibraryDestinationChoice`), reusing the existing choose-multiple-cards + choose-from-list frontend flows
- `RegisterNextDrawLookAtTopReplacementEffect()` — Aladdin's Lamp `{X}, {T}`. One-shot delayed replacement of the controller's **next draw this turn**: reads X from the entry's `xValue`, stores it in `GameData.pendingNextDrawLookAtTop` (per player). `DrawService.resolveDrawCard` consumes it: look at top X, keep the chosen card on top, shuffle the rest to the bottom, then draw it (real draw). Reuses `LibrarySearch` with the `LibrarySearchDestination.DRAW_CHOSEN_REST_TO_BOTTOM_RANDOM` destination (completion + final draw in `LibraryChoiceHandlerService`). Non-targeted; cleared at cleanup ("this turn"). X==1 / small library ⇒ a plain draw
- `EachPlayerShufflesHandAndGraveyardIntoLibraryEffect()` — Timetwister-style
- `EachPlayerShufflesGraveyardIntoLibraryEffect()` — each player shuffles their graveyard into their library (empty GY still shuffles library; Survive)
- `EachPlayerKeepsCardsShufflesRestIntoLibraryEffect(int keepCount)` — each player (APNAP order) chooses up to `keepCount` cards in their hand to keep, shuffles the rest into their library; interactive per-player choice (Worldpurge, `keepCount=7`)
- `EachPlayerLosesUnspentManaEffect()` — each player's mana pool is emptied ("loses all unspent mana"; Worldpurge)
- `AlternatingHandExileEffect()` — target opponent reveals their hand, then that player and the spell's controller alternately exile a card from it (target picks first) until it is empty; the target's picks return to their hand, the controller's picks go to their graveyard. Harmful `PLAYER` spec; alternation carried by `PendingInteraction.AlternatingHandExileChoice`. Struggle for Sanity

## Library manipulation

- `SearchLibraryEffect(DynamicAmount count, CardPredicate filter, LibrarySearchDestination destination, ManaValueBound manaValueBound, int castFromGraveyardCount, boolean requireDifferentNames)` — unified library search (collapsed the `SearchLibraryFor*` family). Convenience: `()` unrestricted-to-hand (Diabolic Tutor), `(filter)` filtered-to-hand, `(filter, destination)`, `(count, filter, destination)`, `(filter, int count, int cfg)` flashback tutor (Increasing Ambition `(null,1,2)`), `(filter, destination, bound)`, `(count, filter, destination, bound, requireDifferentNames)` for Uncage the Menagerie. destination dz??? `HAND`/`BATTLEFIELD`/`BATTLEFIELD_TAPPED`/`TOP_OF_LIBRARY`; by-name via `CardNamedPredicate` (Squadron Hawk); MV bound via filter + `ManaValueBound` — `(exact, offset)` = X-relative (Citanul Flute, Birthing Pod `(true,1)`, Green Sun's Zenith `CardColorPredicate(GREEN)`), or `(DynamicAmount, exact, offset)` for a board-derived bound (Beseech the Queen — `PermanentCount` of lands controlled, null filter). `requireDifferentNames=true` excludes already-chosen names on multi-picks; count 0 still searches and shuffles
- `LibraryOfLatNamEffect()` — SPELL, "an opponent chooses one" of two modes for you: accept schedules a `DrawCardsAtNextUpkeep` delayed draw-3, decline pushes `SearchLibraryEffect()` (tutor to hand). The opponent decides via the may-ability accept/decline prompt (`LibraryOfLatNamEffectHandler` → `MayPenaltyChoiceHandlerService.handleLibraryOfLatNamChoice`). Library of Lat-Nam
- `MisfortuneEffect()` — SPELL, "an opponent chooses one" of two modes for you: accept → +1/+1 counter on each creature you control + gain 4 life; decline → -1/-1 counter on each creature that player controls + 4 damage to them. Opponent decides via the may-ability accept/decline prompt (`MisfortuneEffectHandler` → `MayPenaltyChoiceHandlerService.handleMisfortuneChoice`, which pushes the chosen mode's existing effects onto a stack entry controlled by you). Misfortune
- `CovenantOfMindsEffect()` — SPELL, targets an opponent: reveal top 3 cards of your library; the targeted opponent chooses via the may-ability accept/decline prompt. Accept → the revealed cards go to your hand; decline → they go to your graveyard and you draw five. (`CovenantOfMindsEffectHandler` → `MayPenaltyChoiceHandlerService.handleCovenantOfMindsChoice`). Covenant of Minds
- `IntuitionEffect()` — SPELL, targets an opponent (pair with `PlayerPredicateTargetFilter(OPPONENT)`): you search your library for three cards and reveal them, the targeted opponent chooses one for your hand, the rest go to your graveyard, then you shuffle. Two prompts: `PendingInteraction.IntuitionSearchChoice` (your exactly-three pick) then a `LibraryRevealChoice` for the opponent. Intuition
- `SearchLibraryForBasicLandsToBattlefieldTappedAndHandEffect()` — Cultivate
- `SearchLibraryForCardToHandAndCardToGraveyardEffect()` / `.upToCreaturesRevealed()` — Final Parting (two unrestricted, mandatory) / Jarad's Orders (up to two creatures, revealed; one to hand, other to GY)
- `SearchLibraryForOneCardOfEachColorToHandEffect()` — search for one white, one blue, one black, one red, and one green card (five different cards, WUBRG order); each colour is a separate revealed HAND pick (may fail to find), colours absent from the library are skipped, library shuffled once after the last colour; Conflux
- `SearchLibraryAndGraveyardForNamedCardsToHandEffect(List<String> cardNames)` — search library and graveyard for up to one of each listed name to hand, then shuffle (GY auto-take, library remainder via `namedToHandPicks`; may find any/all/none). Nissa's Encouragement
- `SearchLibraryForInstantsWithManaValuesToHandEffect()` — search for an instant with mana value 3, then 2, then 1 (Firemind's Foresight); each value is a separate revealed HAND pick (may fail to find), absent values skipped, library shuffled once after the last value via `LibrarySearchFollowUp.instantManaValueToHandPicks`
- `TargetPlayerSearchesLibraryForBasicLandToBattlefieldTappedEffect()` — target player searches their library for a basic land card, puts it onto the battlefield tapped, then shuffles; targets a player (player-targeting spec), mandatory search that may fail to find; Fertilid
- `SacrificeAnyNumberOfLandsAndSearchThatManyLandsToBattlefieldTappedEffect()` — controller sacrifices any number of their lands (multi-permanent choice, 0 to all), then searches their library for up to that many land cards to the battlefield tapped, then shuffles; search count = lands sacrificed, may fail to find; Scapeshift
- `SacrificeAnyNumberOfPermanentsThenDrawPerSacrificedEffect(PermanentPredicate filter)` — controller sacrifices any number of their permanents matching `filter` (multi-permanent choice, 0 to all), then draws a card for each one sacrificed; Reprocess (`PermanentAnyOfPredicate` of artifact/creature/land)
- `ChooseFivePermanentsSearchSameNameToBattlefieldTappedEffect()` — controller chooses up to five different permanents they control (multi-permanent choice, capped at 5), then for each may search their library for a same-named card put onto the battlefield tapped, then shuffles; one optional single-name search per chosen permanent; Clarion Ultimatum
- `SearchLibraryForSameNameCreatureForEachControlledCreatureEffect()` — for each creature you control (no choice), you may search your library for a creature card with the same name and put it onto the battlefield untapped, then shuffle; one optional single-name search per creature; Doubling Chant
- `DrawPerSourceCounterThenDamageEffect(CounterType)` — EACH_DRAW_TRIGGERED: the draw-step player draws one extra card per counter of that type on the source, then the source deals that much damage to them; Malignant Growth (`GROWTH`). Implements the `OpponentDrawStepOnlyEffect` marker so the trigger is skipped entirely on the controller's own draw step
- `TargetPlayerLosesLifeAndSearchesLibraryToHandEffect(int lifeLoss)` — EACH_DRAW_TRIGGERED: the draw-step player (`entry.getTargetId()`) loses `lifeLoss` life, then does a mandatory unrestricted tutor of their own library to hand, then shuffles; Maralen of the Mornsong (with static `PlayersCannotDrawCardsEffect()`)
- `SearchLibraryForCurseToBattlefieldAttachedToEnchantedPlayerEffect()` — Curse (name not shared with one already on enchanted player) onto battlefield attached to enchanted player; Curse of Misfortunes
- `SearchLibraryForEquipmentToBattlefieldAndAttachEffect()` — search for an Equipment card, put it onto the battlefield, then choose a creature you control to attach it to, then shuffle; Stonehewer Giant. Controller picks the creature via a follow-up `PermanentChoiceContext.AttachEquipmentToCreature` (no interaction if no creatures)
- `SearchLibraryForAuraToBattlefieldAttachedToTargetCreatureEffect()` — search for an Aura card that could enchant the creature recorded (non-targeting) as the entry's `targetId` (the lone attacker), put it onto the battlefield attached to that creature, then shuffle; Sovereigns of Lost Alara. Wrap in `MayEffect`. Host is fixed (not chosen); uses `LibrarySearchDestination.BATTLEFIELD_ATTACHED_TO_PERMANENT`
- `SearchTargetLibraryEffect(int|DynamicAmount count, CardPredicate filter, LibrarySearchDestination destination, boolean canFailToFind)` — search target player's library for up to `count` cards matching `filter`, move them to `destination`, then that player shuffles. Targets a player; the target-library mirror of `SearchLibraryEffect`. `filter == null` searches the whole library (and exiles face down); `canFailToFind` false = bare quantity (CR 701.23d), true = stated quality or "up to" (CR 701.23b). Destinations: `EXILE` (Jester's Cap `3`/`null`/false, Earwig Squad, Nightmare Incursion `PermanentCount(SWAMP, CONTROLLER)`/`null`/true), `GRAVEYARD` (Life's Finale `3`/`CardTypePredicate(CREATURE)`/true), `BATTLEFIELD_UNDER_SEARCHER` (Bribery — enters under the SEARCHER's control), `EXILE_PLAYABLE` (Praetor's Grasp) and `EXILE_PLAYABLE_UNTIL_NEXT_UPKEEP` (Grinning Totem). A prevented search still shuffles the target's library
- `ChooseCardNameAndExileFromZonesEffect(List<CardType> excludedTypes)` — choose a nonland card name, then exile "any number" of matching cards (player choice) from target player's hand/graveyard/library, then shuffle (Memoricide, Cranial Extraction). Targets player, benign
- `ChooseNameRevealHandDamagePerCopyAndExileEffect(List<CardType> excludedTypes, int damagePerCard)` — choose a card name; target reveals hand; deal `damagePerCard` per copy revealed from hand; exile ALL copies from hand/graveyard/library (mandatory, no choice) and shuffle (Thought Hemorrhage, lands excluded, 3). Targets player, harmful
- `RevealHandChooseCardFromItAndExileAllCopiesEffect(List<CardType> excludedTypes)` — target reveals hand; controller picks a card **in that hand** (excluded types filtered out); exile ALL copies of that name from hand/graveyard/library and shuffle; no damage (Shimian Specter, lands excluded). Targets player, harmful; `CombatDamageTriggerContextEffect` (DAMAGED_PLAYER). Nothing happens when the hand holds no legal card
- `SearchTargetPlayerLibraryAndCastEffect(Set<CardType> castableTypes)` — search target opponent's library for a card of one of the types, caster may cast it without paying its mana cost, then that player shuffles (Knowledge Exploitation, INSTANT/SORCERY). Targets player; uses `CAST_WITHOUT_PAYING`
- `RevealTopCardOfLibraryEffect()` / `(int lifeGainIfLand)` — reveal top card of target player's library; `lifeGainIfLand > 0` gains the controller that much life if it's a land (Prophecy)
- `RevealTopCardGainLifeEqualToManaValueEffect()` — reveal top card of controller's library; gain life = its mana value; card stays on top (Sifter Wurm, after `ScryEffect(3)` ETB)
- `EachPlayerRevealsTopCardLosesLifeEqualToManaValueThenToHandEffect()` — each player (APNAP order) reveals their top card, loses life = its mana value, then puts it into their hand; empty library skipped (Duskmantle Seer, `UPKEEP_TRIGGERED`)
- `RevealTopCardCreatureToBattlefieldElseGraveyardEffect(boolean grantHaste, boolean sacrificeAtEndStep)` — reveal top card; creature → battlefield, otherwise → graveyard (mandatory). No-arg `()` = both false (Call of the Wild `{2}{G}{G}`). `(true, true)` = entering creature gains haste and is sacrificed at the next end step (Impromptu Raid `{2}{R/G}`)
- `RevealTopCardLandToBattlefieldElseToHandEffect()` — reveal top card; land → battlefield, otherwise → hand (mandatory, no choice). Skyward Eye Prophets (`{T}` activated ability)
- `RevealTopCardCreatureTokenLandToBattlefieldElseGainLifeEffect(CreateTokenEffect creatureToken, int lifeGain)` — reveal top card; creature → create `creatureToken`, land → onto the battlefield under the controller's control, noncreature nonland → gain `lifeGain` life. Only the land branch moves the card; empty library does nothing. Implements `TokenCreatingEffect` + `LifeGainEffect`. Druidic Satchel (`{2}, {T}`, 1/1 green Saproling / 2 life)
- `RevealTopCardPutLandsIntoGraveyardRepeatEffect()` — reveal the controller's library one card at a time, binning each land into the graveyard until a non-land (stays on top) or empty (Countryside Crusher, `UPKEEP_TRIGGERED`)
- `RevealTopCardRemoveTargetFromCombatIfMatchEffect(CardPredicate)` — reveal top; if match, remove the engine-set attacking creature (targetId) from combat; then bottom the card (Lost in the Woods, ON_CREATURE_ATTACKS_YOU)
- `RevealTopCardsChosenSubtypeToHandRestToBottomEffect(int count)` — reveal top `count`; creature cards of the source permanent's chosen creature type (Changeling-aware) → hand, rest → bottom in any order (async `LibraryReorder`). Reads `Permanent.getChosenSubtype()`; pair with `ChooseSubtypeOnEnterEffect` (Brass Herald, count=4)
- `LookAtTopCardsRevealTwoTypesToHandThenRestEffect` — look at/reveal top N, may put up to one of each of two card types into hand (two sequential single picks enforce the per-type cap), rest disposed. Factories: `.creatureAndLandToHandRestOnBottom(4)` (Gift of the Gargantuan, private look, rest on bottom) / `.creatureAndEnchantmentToHandRestToGraveyard(5)` (Benefaction of Rhonas, public reveal, rest to graveyard). Second pick runs via `LibrarySearchFollowUp.forSecondBoundedPick`
- `RevealTopCardCreatureGainToughnessLosePowerToHandEffect()` — reveal top; if creature, gain life = toughness, lose life = power, then → hand; non-creature stays on top (Sapling of Colfenor, `ON_ATTACK`)
- `PhyrexianPortalEffect()` - activated ability, targets an opponent: if your library has 10+ cards, that opponent splits your top ten into two face-down piles; you search the pile you pick for a card (to hand, may fail to find), shuffle the rest of it into your library, and the other pile is exiled. Reuses the card-pile flow (`PendingPileSeparation` with `CardPileDisposition.SEARCH_ONE_TO_HAND`) plus a `PendingPortalPileSearch`-marked `LibraryRevealChoice` (Phyrexian Portal)
- `RevealTopCardsAndSeparateEffect(int count)` — Fact-or-Fiction: reveal top `count` of your library; an opponent separates them into two piles; controller chooses one pile → hand, the other → graveyard. Non-targeting. Reuses the card-pile flow (`PendingPileSeparation` with `CardPileDisposition.HAND`); handled by `RevealTopCardsAndSeparateEffectHandler` + `GraveyardReturnSupport` step 1/2. Used by Unesh, Criosphinx Sovereign's enters trigger
- `RevealTopCardsBottomThenDamageIfCopyRevealedEffect(int count, int damage)` — reveal top `count`, bottom them in any order (async `LibraryReorder`); if a card sharing the source's name was revealed, deal `damage` to the any-target (Stomping Slabs 7/7). Any-target chosen on cast; no damage if no copy revealed
- `ScryEffect(int)` / `ScryEffect(DynamicAmount)` — scry N (or dynamic X)
- `SurveilEffect(int)` — surveil N
- `LookAtTopCardPutLandOrCreatureWithinLoyaltyEffect()` — look at the controller's top card (private); if it's a land, or a creature whose mana value ≤ the loyalty counters on the source permanent, the controller **may** put it onto the battlefield (else it stays on top). Nissa, Steward of Elements 0. Loyalty threshold read from `entry.getSourcePermanentId()` at resolution; the "may" runs via a paired mayfx handler
- `LookAtTopCardMayPutMatchingOntoBattlefieldEffect(CardPredicate)` — look at the controller's top card (private); if it matches the predicate, the controller **may** put it onto the battlefield (else it stays on top). Into the Wilds uses `CardTypePredicate(LAND)`; the "may" runs via a paired mayfx handler
- `LookAtTopCardMayPutCreatureOntoBattlefieldElseToHandEffect()` — look at top (private); if creature, **may** put onto battlefield; if declined or non-creature, put into hand. Believe (Reason // Believe aftermath). Paired mayfx handler
- `LookAtTopCardMayRevealMatchingToHandEffect(CardPredicate, boolean mayGraveyardOtherwise)` — private look at top; if matching, may reveal to hand. `mayGraveyardOtherwise = true` also offers a graveyard may for a card that stays out of hand (Archghoul of Thraben, `CardSubtypePredicate(ZOMBIE)`); `false` leaves it on top (Domri Rade +1, `CardTypePredicate(CREATURE)`). Two-stage may (`Stage.MAY_HAND` / `MAY_GRAVEYARD`)
- `ShuffleLibraryEffect(boolean targetPlayer)` — shuffle library (false=controller's, true=target player's)
- `LookAtTopCardsExileRandomRestOnTopEffect(int count, int exileCount)` — look at the top `count` cards of your library, exile `exileCount` of them **at random** (engine picks, not the player), then put the rest back on top in any order (async `LibraryReorder` with `toBottom=false` when 2+ remain). Orcish Librarian `(8, 4)`. A short library just exiles everything it can
- `LookAtTopCardsOfTargetLibraryEffect(DynamicAmount count, TargetLibraryAction action)` (convenience `int` overload) — look at top N of target player's library, then apply the action: LOOK_ONLY (pure peek, Dewdrop/Orcish Spy), MAY_EXILE_ONE (Psychic Surgery; no target = own library, Puresight Merrow), MAY_SHUFFLE (Visions count=5, benign PLAYER target, reuses `ShuffleLibraryEffect(true)` may-handler), PUT_ONE_INTO_GRAVEYARD (Cruel Fate, Wu Spy), EXILE_ONE (mandatory exile of one, rest on top in any order — Sealed Fate, count `new XValue()`), MAY_PUT_TOP_ON_BOTTOM (Coral Fighters, Precognition; count=1, harmful PLAYER target — the card is shown in a may-ability prompt and, if accepted, moved to the bottom via `PutTopCardOfTargetLibraryOnBottomEffect`)
- `PutTopCardOfTargetLibraryOnBottomEffect()` — move the top card of the stack entry's `targetId` player's library to the bottom of that library. Declares a benign PLAYER `TargetSpec` so the may-ability accept path carries the pre-chosen player onto the stack entry; only used as the body of `LookAtTopCardsOfTargetLibraryEffect(1, MAY_PUT_TOP_ON_BOTTOM)`
- `ExileSpellEffect()` — exile this spell instead of putting it into the graveyard after resolution (marker). Pair with Miracle / Recall
- `MiracleCast("{cost}")` — casting option (not an effect): declare on card; DrawService offers reveal on first draw; trigger may cast for cost (CR 702.94). See Temporal Mastery
- `ShuffleIntoLibraryEffect()` — shuffle spell into library
- `ShuffleSelfAndGraveyardIntoLibraryEffect()` — shuffle self + graveyard into library
- `ShuffleSelfFromGraveyardIntoLibraryEffect()` — triggered ability: shuffle the source card from its owner's graveyard into their library (pair with `ON_SELF_PUT_INTO_GRAVEYARD_FROM_ANYWHERE`, e.g. Purity)
- `ShuffleGraveyardIntoLibraryEffect(boolean targetPlayer)` — shuffle graveyard into library (targetPlayer=true targets, false=controller's)
- `ShuffleTargetCardsFromGraveyardIntoLibraryEffect(CardPredicate, int)` — shuffle N cards from graveyard
- `ShuffleTargetCardsFromControllerGraveyardIntoLibraryEffect(CardPredicate, int)` — shuffle up to N from controller's own graveyard (ETB multi-target; Ghostly Castigator)
- `ShuffleCardFromControllerGraveyardIntoLibraryEffect(CardPredicate)` — "you may shuffle up to one card from your graveyard into your library"; resolution-time optional single-card choice from controller's own graveyard (non-targeted, pairs with `CounterSpellEffect`; Put Away)
- `ShuffleTargetPermanentIntoLibraryEffect()` — target permanent's owner shuffles it into their library (Deglamer; constrain to artifact/enchantment etc. via the card's target filter)
- `ShuffleSelfIntoOwnerLibraryEffect()` — non-targeted: the source permanent's owner shuffles it into their library (Blitz Hellion's `END_STEP_TRIGGERED`). Acts on the stack entry's source permanent via `removePermanentToLibraryShuffled`
- `CastTopOfLibraryWithoutPayingManaCostEffect(Set<CardType>)` — cast top free
